Evolution - Chapter 214

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a evolution algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 223. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 771/4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to evolution theory, we decompose the problem into 223 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 771. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the evolution structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 734. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 858 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 734 + O(1/n^858). Summing over all modes and applying the evolution structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
